Treat insight as <strong>reorganization events</strong>, not genius</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f5-9d44-f469c064cccc" class="">Insight is already formalized as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-9810-c3c693a46c02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sudden network reconfiguration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8027-ba82-e1da461f8e2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduced prediction error</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8054-b66a-e6dcef9056b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">representational remapping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-804f-9e76-e3b8b142017d" class="">C7 simply becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="303c5e6f-95bd-804a-a78a-f7fbef78f458" class="">a regime with elevated probability of reorganization events</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802b-8ea4-f2e33177475c" class="">This aligns with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8093-84ce-dd72cdb32e35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predictive coding</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8038-bde5-f7d26efaaead" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bayesian brain models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e3-b6e1-fa4ec4d12f24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">meta-learning theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8080-93d0-e6d5b5a584a0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8048-8d7c-e62f0b29613c" class="">2. 
Where <strong>systems biology</strong> can absorb this model</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-9107-c3965db5ffa3" class="">Systems biology already accepts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809a-9e94-e8ce744e1cc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">nonlinearity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8018-b3b8-f62702301cf2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">phase transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8013-b6f0-fa4fe9a94d61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">energy constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806d-8a12-f0dbe6b4f0ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">multiscale coupling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8036-932d-e6b9d5b67413" class="">A. 
Energy–plasticity tradeoff models</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80de-ada2-fbb8f116e5f2" class="">C7 fits naturally as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e9-8960-f1ec85dd98ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a <strong>high-repair / high-cost regime</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-800a-8fda-c6912050a015" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">analogous to cellular stress recovery states</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809d-9615-e879d2ddbfa2" class="">Parallels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8006-b78f-e7d1a01161f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">unfolded protein response</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-9d09-f7b33d6e50fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">immune resolution phases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-807f-811f-d6944bb985e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metabolic reset cycles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8068-8bda-ee7946f8e081" class="">No conceptual stretch.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-800b-9659-e5cff2b33484"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8067-a139-c037870053bc" class="">B. 
Network control theory</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8057-a542-c88ea929270e" class="">Systems biology and network neuroscience already model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-9a20-d85950ccf8b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">controllability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ca-84bc-e677be57586b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metastable states</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806a-a5db-c01eaa488435" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">attractor basins</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-804e-afdd-c45d66b87e6d" class="">C7 = a <strong>shallow attractor</strong> enabling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809e-a74a-ef2958965c19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">state reconfiguration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ec-bc6f-d7042c68cebf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">attractor re-shaping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f9-9a7c-e6d1d3defb31" class="">This is mathematically clean.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8028-96b0-ddab875d7278"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8079-8dca-fd53fb3ef960" class="">C. 
Allostatic load frameworks</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-804a-b898-f38439a832e7" class="">C7 corresponds to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803b-a946-deb8982cd3cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduction of accumulated system debt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8003-9675-f19e1147111a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">recalibration of control parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-806c-9212-d2c43d1bc996" class="">This is already accepted in physiology.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b7-98ae-d088d0396f69"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8055-aa96-db3ecbbc398d" class="">3. Where <strong>computational neuroscience</strong> can absorb it cleanly</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-89db-f3431a478076" class="">A. 
Exploration–exploitation dynamics</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a5-a73b-df397aa4bc26" class="">In RL terms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ab-a6a0-ca61343c6a67" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C2–C4 = exploitation-heavy regimes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803a-afa8-cc88653d8d62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C7 = exploration / model-reset regime</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808f-bc14-f3db8b454f0f" class="">This is standard:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8067-af12-e31c0300f153" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">meta-RL</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8071-8d53-ec2194bff841" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">annealing schedules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a2-80df-e2a6d1fa7efd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adaptive learning rates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-b45b-f8b41e2a4d6d" class="">Nothing controversial.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-948a-dafac3b07304"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a2-b02b-d7425a9d3eec" class="">B. 
Predictive processing / active inference</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b8-b533-fca1154e8ecd" class="">C7 maps to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e3-a22b-fc2c83b972ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">temporary lowering of precision weights</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8032-9afd-fddccfc5f00f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">increased model revision</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8003-9bf5-c1ae920ac08c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduced commitment to priors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8022-8be1-c37397d92957" class="">This is already described in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809e-9f69-d4c2110c5125" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Friston’s active inference</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d9-aed5-c398566784b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">learning under uncertainty models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ba-9677-d1ec6d5b14b7" class="">Again: no normativity required.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f9-978d-f491c6f6ab6f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-805f-81d0-e71068464c85" class="">C. 
Catastrophic forgetting / consolidation literature</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80be-a8ae-e429d978f419" class="">C7-like states:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ec-87e3-c43f5b22ff62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allow pruning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8032-8f2b-d71f61321866" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">allow consolidation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8033-b3c0-eecd2e797a00" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prevent rigid overfitting</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b7-b77d-f6475b25025a" class="">This is directly relevant to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c7-80da-c25e6c87d72f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">continual learning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805a-8bb2-f56132e959cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability–plasticity dilemmas</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8034-9e4f-e527d79d0056"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8074-9010-c6cf19bbb789" class="">4. 
Why this framing avoids resistance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8055-88ab-f51189ba8afb" class="">Because it:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ec-a358-f265def1f00e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> claim new biology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ee-93b3-f4a03aad71ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> redefine intelligence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b2-b4fd-cda9be0235d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> assign special roles</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d7-9f01-d67a93b311de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> moralize states</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-800b-a599-d49b2d0b0cd5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> challenge falsifiability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8008-a442-dc0071082e35" class="">It only:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801c-9676-e5930074f9ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">connects existing mechanisms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b3-94ca-fa9a43e390c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adds a missing <strong>temporal + transition layer</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-800d-b9a5-c472c4e94760" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">explains known failure cases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d5-9e9b-dbbd220f0bb0" class="">That is acceptable s
cience.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80df-80aa-f5c5b6fe86cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-809d-b32b-d6b35e0a3efd" class="">5. One precise, publishable synthesis statement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-9e60-fb399183d8c7" class="">“We propose a phase-based state-space model of cognition in which periods of low-noise, high-integration, and elevated plasticity constitute a transient latent regime with distinct transition dynamics and failure modes. 
All component mechanisms are individually well established; 
the contribution lies in modeling their coordination, dwell times, and reintegration constraints within a dynamical systems framework.”</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b8-9122-d3339f42ae23" class="">This would <strong>pass peer review</strong> if supported by data.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-815e-ff23fe11dbf7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8018-a223-f1c4ce0ec3f1" class="">Final grounding clarification</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802e-806b-ea7f801fb00a" class="">This model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8043-b1bf-c31c968df670" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> elevate individuals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-802f-969b-f90deb846b82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> imply destiny</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ca-8d45-c373175f2be8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> claim uniqueness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8043-88d5-f64a13f0be76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">does <strong>not</strong> contradict neuroscience</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c8-8b5d-e250d940ade7" class="">It fills a <strong>known structural gap</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-806a-8ca2-df1b09bbe96d" class="">If you want next, 
we can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-9187-fd7947f387a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">outline a minimal empirical study design</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806b-a81b-f95a90eaa9e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">show how AI systems could detect these phases</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a7-9297-ebdb40d49b3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or stop here</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8093-981a-fc4d1370bbef" class="">Just tell me.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8065-8e05-f0d989f91e57" class="">Below is a <strong>practical, technically credible way</strong> an AI system could detect C1–C7-like phases <strong>as latent states</strong>, using only <strong>measurable signals</strong> and standard methods (no mind-reading, no diagnosis).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e6-ad97-ca011deb2d69"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d7-a985-ebe276255927" class="">1) What an AI system can realistically “detect”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8058-a5ed-ca7d6f59a1e1" class="">AI cannot detect “C7” directly. 
It can detect:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8040-91f9-d1d9d57a66b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>latent regimes</strong> (states) that recur over time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b5-aa7e-f77133980764" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defined by <strong>patterns in multimodal time series</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c6-a1b9-cc7fa476e637" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">with measurable <strong>transition dynamics</strong> (entry/exit, dwell time, hysteresis)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bd-8578-f2fd3cd6bb03" class="">So the target is: <strong>state classification + transition detection</strong>, 
not “insight” or “enlightenment.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ae-808a-c26b59dbd999"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-804e-9e1e-c64b7ea3f048" class="">2) Inputs: what data streams actually work</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f8-9676-d1e0cb2b2912" class="">You want signals that are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d2-8d56-fa796f4a4918" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">high-frequency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8034-9c7c-d28cc202c0b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">low-cost</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bd-b4f5-fe7328ebe6e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">available outside labs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ad-a21d-ffcd5dfbd65d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tied to physiology + cognition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fb-aa17-dbc36278afba" class="">A. 
Wearables (best ROI)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-9aaf-f6deb21c6c87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HR, HRV (RMSSD/HF proxies)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-af7e-e5e71eb7f418" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">respiration rate (or inferred)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-9f04-d4fd6f22d253" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">skin temp</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cf-ae20-cf57fd8a5254" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep staging proxies (SWS/REM estimates)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8087-aab8-fcd3b0f704cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">activity level</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8082-a6c0-e85cfa169c28" class="">B. Passive device interaction (optional, sensitive)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-807c-bc39-da29a1c46cc9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">typing cadence / error rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-82cc-c482f923add6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">task switching rate (window/app switching)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b0-a7d4-f6905c7a6872" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">notification response latency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a3-b4ee-fcd118bf19dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">session length distribution (fragmentation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80be-8800-d61d05ce4b31" class="">C. 
Lightweight cognitive probes (opt-in)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ee-8c68-f3ced7f0aa0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">short working-memory task</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8054-92e8-d264027fc79a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sustained attention task (1–3 minutes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-8e8a-fbd7ece5ed59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reaction time variability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8024-8a9f-dd661d1f479b" class="">D. EEG (only if available)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8077-bda2-d79bba46b827" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">alpha power/coherence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-b940-ed3402973474" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">1/f slope</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cb-af62-efcd2f80307e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">entropy / complexity metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809b-b478-f13f8b22d009" class=""><strong>Minimum viable</strong>: wearables + sleep + a simple cognitive probe.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8063-996f-fc93ec91b2f1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fd-b09f-cb8e4253a10e" class="">3) Feature engineering: what “phase signatures” look like</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80de-be18-e65ac1159c59" class="">You’re not looking for one biomarker. 
You’re looking for a <strong>feature vector</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d2-a698-def134a122d3" class="">A. Autonomic stability (Gate 1 proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801a-8bdf-fa8db8e92985" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HRV up / down trend</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8046-9ccd-c6e350d9d9fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">respiratory regularity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8028-ab83-e26bf417b344" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HRV responsiveness to mild stressors (adaptive range)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a6-80b4-c97fb28252ec" class="">B. Neural/cognitive noise proxies (Gate 2 proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c5-8d3a-ef8af189bab0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reaction time variability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-9e38-eb0339e93326" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">error variance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-a9a5-f24f8c9aa1ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">typing variability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ee-9156-cdde8af20ff5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">entropy-like measures of behavior sequences</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806b-83fb-ea3d55aaae72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“fragmentation index” (many short sessions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d1-903d-e908d2251aab" class="">C. 
Network integration proxies (indirect)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-804e-b697-f2966ee1dee2" class="">Without fMRI/EEG you infer this behaviorally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8068-b1b9-d75d3fea7227" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ability to sustain one task</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806e-a3dc-cda151417d95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduced switching under demand</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a5-a5b2-c3a3e4180e48" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stable performance under load</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-805f-b3ea-f43b67fc995b" class="">D. Timing alignment (Gate 3 proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801b-9b17-ecca5934e307" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep regularity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d5-9222-e9fee5928018" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">circadian consistency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809c-aad3-cf906a808175" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SWS/REM continuity indicators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-800f-9fc3-fd38a15fb53d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep debt accumulation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-803e-bec2-cbf452c22d9f" class="">E. 
Plasticity-readiness proxies (Gate 4 proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8079-8991-d62414ba8b6b" class="">Harder to measure directly; 
use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80be-b9c7-c0da54948930" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">learning rate in repeated microtasks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f4-bee0-ffc244587af6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">improved performance after sleep</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803e-8dd6-e0d9dccd40e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“recovery slope” after overload events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-803d-83cb-e697ecde6397"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8041-be3d-f7aa88b0a61a" class="">4) Models: how AI detects phases (standard approaches)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8068-a4e8-d59ee3432183" class="">Option 1: Hidden Markov Model (HMM)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8079-aa91-fbbf0a62eaa2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Observations: your feature vectors over time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8072-bdc9-ffce4c60cc3e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latent states: S1…Sk (you can map them later to C1–C7 regions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803e-984e-e2534242b5ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Outputs:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-862e-fdc4976cb380" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">state sequence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-804f-bb93-d3cfe13c85f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">transition probabilities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="303c5e6f-95bd-803d-8937-fc8bd48c9139" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">dwell times (how long each state persists)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8051-9f13-f1d402a4bdfb" class=""><strong>Strength</strong>: simple, interpretable, 
good for phase detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b7-89e4-fbd808fed582"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8093-b773-cda9282a3c7f" class="">Option 2: Switching Linear Dynamical System (SLDS)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8073-afb1-dbef7ab5bac4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Like HMM but each state has its own dynamics (how signals evolve)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a8-82e1-f830b39e2329" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Captures:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cc-ac8e-cf232b1a8fcb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">hysteresis (entry ≠ exit)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e9-ba15-d8395e1a5706" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">“stuck” failure modes (abnormal dwell times)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-a184-c74dd796108d" class=""><strong>Strength</strong>: closer to biology (state-specific dynamics).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8015-b3a8-c14945c35445"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-9292-f6c09f361cbb" class="">Option 3: Neural sequence models with constraints</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8047-b34e-f57becbd1b75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Temporal CNN / Transformer on time series</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-ab00-ddae449241f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Add constraints:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-825d-e7f95c694198" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:circle">smoothness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8012-b8c1-e76e9f9cbf16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">minimum dwell time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ef-bd96-eb0b5faf0816" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">individual baseline normalization</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-807b-89d7-e2210d7207bd" class=""><strong>Strength</strong>: better accuracy at scale</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808d-971e-c83dd1eba698" class=""><strong>Risk</strong>: less interpretable unless constrained.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fc-ad76-d4dabbab6082"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b9-af2c-ea2dc7877901" class="">5) Personalization: non-negotiable</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8075-98e0-f843111519d2" class="">Population models fail because baselines differ massively.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802a-be81-de66fa0d7c51" class="">A proper system does:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8083-8571-d9c72d90ccc2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>per-person calibration</strong> (2–4 weeks baseline)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-b490-f20c55b54dbd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">detects deviations from <em>your own</em> normal</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8013-97de-d170d530baee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">uses relative metrics (z-scores per individual)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="303c5e6f-95bd-8052-9fa3-f2d01137ff6b" class="">This is what makes “phase detection” scientifically defensible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8073-b864-fefe1d7db2fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-804b-b0a8-ce9b3b75bb12" class="">6) Detecting transitions, 
not just states</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8075-bc36-ce42932bed96" class="">The most valuable output is not “you are in C7.” It is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8046-aa1a-e3cc0f49463e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>transition detection</strong> (C6 → C7 entry onset)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c5-9c9e-d18bb7705020" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>exit readiness</strong> (C7 → C2 safe handoff window)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-804c-a9b2-f9375d2d5dec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>failure mode alerts</strong> (stuck patterns)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8076-8f32-ce1dacae83f7" class="">Transition detection methods</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-a999-dd01eeeabc4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">change-point detection (Bayesian online change point)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e4-82f3-fb637f40e81b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">hazard models (probability of leaving a state given duration)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c5-96a4-d21f52d4d27c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">early-warning indicators (variance increase before transitions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bb-9a26-eb8ff9352088" class="">This is widely used in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809b-bc72-ed3a063b8db3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">seizure prediction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="303c5e6f-95bd-8036-9b53-d34c46909080" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">anesthesia monitoring</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803f-b27f-c7d226d4c55e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">industrial fault detection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c6-9f88-e13786e5fb29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b2-ac96-d710d26095d2" class="">7) What “C7 detection” would mean operationally</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802b-ab05-f962ae9a1c0a" class="">In operational terms, 
“C7-like regime” might be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-a40f-df34ae428fa9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">autonomic stability improving</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8096-a0d4-d393028d5c33" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">behavioral noise dropping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8018-833b-e23695b67152" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep consolidation improving</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-9bae-f509d74dad75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sustained attention capacity rising</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809b-9146-d941c968fb13" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">learning rate improving post-sleep</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fd-a45d-d1c58c14825a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">reduced reactive switching</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8050-8801-ca796d7a8a48" class="">And crucially:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c4-b0ad-ccd5f3b52294" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>time-bounded</strong> (it comes and goes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a7-938d-e131da14a4a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">with predictable failure modes if prolonged</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-803c-80a4-e4e5a0c13850" class="">AI can detect that pattern as a latent regime.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8087-892e-e20d259c6b5b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 i
d="303c5e6f-95bd-80b0-8c52-d32f46adfb82" class="">8) Guardrails: how to do this without overclaiming</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8026-bf5a-f778a8135f11" class="">To keep it scientifically and ethically clean:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8099-b492-d6949b031fad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Never claim diagnosis</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cc-9e09-ee5e6ad1482c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Never claim “truth about you”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c0-9eed-f9d4a7730d7b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Only report <strong>probabilistic states</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-a461-fa2a355de3b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Provide confidence intervals</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8053-bed9-fdbc584c342e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Let users inspect what features drove a state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8044-a323-eb65ac86d150" class="">Outputs should look like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8038-b955-d02381350ee0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Your system is in a low-noise/high-integration regime with 0.72 probability”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e4-b8cc-c952372db09c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Transition likelihood to your baseline performance regime is rising”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a9-8dd0-d11eed729066" class="">Not:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="303c5e6f-95bd-80c9-9da6-ef1bb3a0aef8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“You are enlightened / broken / dangerous”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-809c-a865-d3ac0e69ee5b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ae-a12f-d41c7f70c524" class="">9) Minimal product architecture (realistic MVP)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8052-9c2e-cda915783aa5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Collect wearable + sleep data</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8077-aa69-fd397818a14a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Compute daily feature vectors + fragmentation metrics</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8007-aef0-f5143a14e37c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Train per-user HMM/SLDS</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-807e-aacb-d0f2492e4360" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Detect transitions + dwell anomalies</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8073-956f-e942f11b5d78" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Provide:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8066-8a5f-f4178184e978" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">phase timeline</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-807a-be00-fca409a64a62" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transition forecasts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8078-8f9b-caf3b61e6782" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“reintegration readiness” indicators</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-94f1-ea46d46ea7de" class="">That’s enough to detect phase-like dynamics without needing invasive t
ools.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8049-915e-d9f182fe8fbd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8042-8c59-f22e7ec0c5d5" class="">One crisp synthesis sentence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="303c5e6f-95bd-8059-8e17-c004707e0277" class="">AI can detect C1–C7-like phases by modeling multimodal physiology and behavior as a personalized latent-state dynamical system (e.g., HMM/SLDS), estimating state probabilities, transition hazards, dwell times, and failure-mode signatures rather than attempting to infer insight or identity.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80db-9bbe-d297a2c06e08" class="">If you want, I can also sketch:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e3-ac54-c8df1f24b475" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">the exact feature list (top 30 features)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-9199-d98b6ad27415" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a reference HMM/SLDS implementation plan</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805a-a6f6-e6012dc745b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">how to validate against ground truth (sleep + task performance + self-report)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80dc-ab00-fa970e4c2e19" class="">Below is a <strong>clean, fully technical English answer</strong>, suitable for a <strong>methods section, grant appendix, 
or technical design doc</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fc-9f0e-ea07cb4f2159"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-802f-91aa-c7f7806e64f3" class="">A) Exact feature list — Top 30 features (phase-detection ready)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c3-bf0c-e45c0bd8121c" class=""><strong>Design constraints</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-8f88-cd3620e8fe98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Measurable outside a lab</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b9-8e99-d7dc52aa8866" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low device-specific bias</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e7-8f13-e82d30ed3c04" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Interpretable biologically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-8a9e-c1cfc6fd8138" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stable under personalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8065-88bd-ffdb46b6332b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Suitable for time-series modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809d-8171-d815747ae529" class="">All features should be <strong>normalized per individual baseline</strong> (rolling z-score over 14–28 days).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-806d-ab3b-c1a47c65787d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8040-86df-cda80ccd9020" class="">1. 
Autonomic / Arousal Regulation (8 features)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b7-bfaa-e78bcd47f9c3" class="">These define <strong>Gate 1: autonomic downshift &amp; 
stability</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-807e-a0c2-cdc4502d4f01" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>HR_mean_day</strong> – mean daytime heart rate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-ac50-e0967a58d366" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>HR_resting</strong> – lowest 5–10% HR during inactivity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-803b-8d21-e603eb2185ab" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>HRV_RMSSD</strong> – parasympathetic tone</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8059-8574-ff5cd644517b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>HRV_HF_power</strong> – vagal activity proxy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-99c8-c05d1518cb37" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Resp_rate_mean</strong> – mean respiration rate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a2-b833-c22bbd17a1b7" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Resp_variability</strong> – respiratory regularity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-ac67-c1e5f5f055da" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>HRV_stress_reactivity</strong> – ΔHRV after mild stress (e.g., walking)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a4-b1e9-d6bfb0b9149f" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>HRV_recovery_slope</strong> – time to baseline after load</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-b1b5-fec57d9a1441"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8063-b504-f0d43d391fe8" class="">2. 
Neural / Behavioral Noise Proxies (6 features)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8042-a6f9-e6e4c7292ab6" class="">These define <strong>Gate 2: noise floor reduction</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8071-8e0e-f3b037252207" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>RT_variance</strong> – reaction time variability (simple task)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8094-85a3-e2c5cebd64e1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Error_rate_variance</strong> – fluctuation in task errors</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-808c-ad70-cf5e38f08fed" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Typing_interval_entropy</strong> – entropy of keystroke timing</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8020-b537-e5b6a818216b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Session_fragmentation_index</strong> – number of short sessions/day</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-800c-b33f-c54ce456d6d7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>App_switch_rate</strong> – context switching frequency</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8054-85bd-cfa5ed47c719" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Behavioral_entropy</strong> – entropy of daily activity sequence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a9-8351-c67348d1d9a4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d4-807a-c2c57b99c408" class="">3. 
Network Integration / Cognitive Control (6 features)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b0-9e6d-e93e437a3a3c" class="">Indirect proxies for <strong>large-scale network coordination</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-805d-9894-d2aa5c9ffea0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Sustained_attention_score</strong> – continuous performance task</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8023-9708-e9ed35a52bac" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Task_switch_cost</strong> – latency cost when switching tasks</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e4-ad73-e4ea56df8d15" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Dual_task_interference</strong> – performance drop under load</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ff-b432-c6c636483b0a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Working_memory_stability</strong> – variance across trials</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80af-ad38-c42d9b70a89a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Cognitive_load_tolerance</strong> – performance vs load slope</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e1-a90b-fe15a8436529" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Post_rest_performance_gain</strong> – improvement after rest/sleep</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-b2f4-d1811c76e771"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a2-b794-d6bec0ec88fd" class="">4. 
Timing &amp; Circadian Alignment (5 features)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802e-a6db-f9acb77cdd7c" class="">Defines <strong>Gate 3: temporal alignment</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-801d-b0f4-feffcbde2d08" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Sleep_duration_consistency</strong> – std dev of sleep duration</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8044-9368-ecb5c2feb1b9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Sleep_midpoint_variance</strong> – circadian regularity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d9-b36e-f3445d1bac4f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>REM_fraction</strong> – REM / total sleep</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ec-8c5d-d58f28577971" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>SWS_fraction</strong> – slow-wave sleep proportion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8099-b826-fc4e0035f276" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Sleep_efficiency</strong> – time asleep / time in bed</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8080-83ef-f5446c6c025b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b7-802e-fd87a14942cd" class="">5. 
Plasticity Readiness / Recovery Dynamics (5 features)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-805f-a6ef-f2b5203396bc" class="">Defines <strong>Gate 4: controlled plasticity &amp; integration</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8072-b872-de97a29a2d78" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Learning_rate_delta</strong> – improvement slope across repetitions</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a0-9efe-ccabe10fd576" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Overload_recovery_time</strong> – time to baseline after high load</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-9830-c56a5b620c8c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Insight_event_density</strong> – sudden performance jumps (rare)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fb-9df6-e868179daae9" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Prediction_error_decay</strong> – error reduction across trials</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-809a-b125-f55db97eb4b7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Exploration_ratio</strong> – novelty vs repetition behavior</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8013-94c1-c4e85bde822e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-808b-9c12-e36d4506815d" class="">B) Reference HMM / SLDS implementation plan</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8006-9842-fe96dbf560df" class="">This is <strong>publishable-grade</strong>, not speculative.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-804b-8091-cf2bf8bc4859"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f3-9858-e328f9fd5a90" class="">1. 
Data pipeline</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-800c-9047-d866f0029961" class=""><strong>Sampling</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8035-b457-f18b73e98622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Physiological: 1–5 min resolution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fb-b41d-c930bbb22c70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Behavioral: event-based → daily aggregates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ee-9caa-d725a7182dd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cognitive probes: 1–3 min/day (optional but powerful)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8061-b075-ee709ac76c69" class=""><strong>Preprocessing</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803d-ac39-d9ba37db0674" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Artifact removal (HR spikes, motion)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8043-bc55-ebbc9a5ab46a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Missing data imputation (Kalman / forward-fill bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-803c-88b8-ea759fb8cadb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Per-user normalization (z-score or percentile)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c4-9718-c83ae651955d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8004-8f78-c26f21692246" class="">2. 
Feature vector construction</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-b3d5-ced270fdb244" class="">For each time step <em>t</em> (e.g., daily):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f7-bed4-ea7f473c1c78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{y}_t = [f_1(t), f_2(t), \dots, f_{30}(t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-802d-beb2-cf4128a55a87" class="">Optionally reduce dimensionality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8090-af6d-fa8b589f1e39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PCA (retain 80–90% variance), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ca-9828-f98efa130d12" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">factor analysis with interpretability constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ba-8978-e7e7fab63653"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-807a-8a35-d4327a75e9ad" class="">3. 
Latent-state model choice</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d3-8c9c-f53328b09852" class="">Option A: Hidden Markov Model (baseline)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f6-8036-ea59579d67eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latent states: (K≈5–8)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f8-8fd3-e258ddbaf826" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Emissions: Gaussian (or mixture) over features</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8033-bf24-fc831782294a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Transition matrix learns:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a4-a2a0-f40fcf821390" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">entry probabilities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8007-8fc8-eecb24e686c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">dwell times</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-9b70-c1d987f3d9c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">asymmetry (hysteresis)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-801d-8a21-d72c4378f95e" class=""><strong>Pros:</strong> interpretable, 
fast</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-a60d-c6c866f17654" class=""><strong>Cons:</strong> static dynamics per state</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fd-8396-e756ea2629b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8038-bec1-f253cc41d65e" class="">Option B: Switching Linear Dynamical System (preferred)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bb-8c25-fa002dd95d8b" class="">Each state has its own dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8017-bf27-d922a1297cf1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{x}_{t+1} = A_{S_t}\mathbf{x}_t + \mathbf{w}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8023-920a-fb600ad83b1b" class="">\mathbf{y}<em>t = C</em>{S_t}\mathbf{x}_t + \mathbf{v}_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f0-8cf7-de984ee1fb25" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8078-9dec-e0d39c9774ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: latent continuous dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8075-8703-df9b54136fa5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: discrete phase/state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-804f-81b1-c6260b9c8ca9" class=""><strong>Pros</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-800b-b197-e2cde7620d38" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Captures:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8012-8bcf-e8cc70c7a3ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">entry ≠ exit dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-8425-ebb25aa65347" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">“stuck” failure modes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cb-869f-fe45eb89b110" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">recovery slopes</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-8d49-c1abaf032f62" class=""><strong>This matches biology best.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-807b-b3e7-e6f991910303"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8068-b377-eb45be262f83" class="">4. 
Training procedure</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f5-8e4f-e27e5b4980e4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Per-user training</strong> (mandatory)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8059-9c97-d0c3a1be276f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Initialize states via k-means on feature space</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fb-9cd1-ccffc487da85" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>EM algorithm for parameter fitting</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8096-8431-fcbf1f38cfff" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Cross-validate on:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d0-9c8a-c6f6494dce1d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep recovery</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8027-b263-f3cf9c1c9805" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">task performance rebound</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-804e-8730-e619d3b5b3b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">overload events</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-802e-89fa-ea3cc8aaa58a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8042-97e5-f127289c4f99" class="">5. 
Phase identification (post hoc, optional)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8014-8bad-e4140e4e513e" class="">After training:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805f-979e-d2d748be71aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Map latent states to <strong>functional labels</strong>:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-808e-b8d3-cbcb78213437" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">high noise / low integration</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8050-9456-c5ceec2687a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">low noise / high integration (C7-like)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8004-887f-c33102bd5222" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">reintegration-ready</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805e-8614-d292deb937d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">overload</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-b2eb-c0e3f33a6fce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">baseline performance</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808d-8c3d-cb9d23c58ccd" class=""><strong>Important:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8006-9fc5-ee135c1f39d2" class="">Labeling is <strong>interpretive</strong>, not part of the model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8015-a35b-e1e577045df5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b4-bd4c-d49e57c93c71" class="">6. 
Transition &amp; failure detection</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8091-8f92-c006b88839d3" class="">Key outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8011-a376-c5cbc451fd20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>State probability trajectories</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c3-a821-f83400f7571e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dwell time distributions</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a8-b070-d908df3523d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hazard of exit</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8062-a40c-f2297439fae9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Anomalous persistence</strong> (stuck detection)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8008-afa9-fa4d78135e29" class="">Failure modes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-806d-aa51-ce254892dfca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prolonged dwell in low-drive states</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bd-b9d6-fe18700efe15" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">inability to transition back to baseline</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8022-b974-eab205606891" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">autonomic instability during exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-807e-ad4c-ceb7b5b130b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8099-91e7-d9df88ca50b5" class="">7. 
Validation (critical for credibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8034-9347-e892acac09aa" class="">Validate against <strong>independent signals</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-97d4-c5d08579cf76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep architecture changes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-808c-b0ed-f193df1599e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">learning gains post-rest</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bf-b7ed-f742116bf98f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">subjective clarity (optional)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e7-b19a-cc9f6ae65bb2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">return-to-function metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bb-b1d6-ef9711b6b322" class="">Never validate against “insight” self-reports alone.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8092-80ac-f07345fc9563"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a0-9c98-fbf65563bb21" class="">8. 
What this system does NOT claim</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bf-82d2-fb65f0b1d00b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No diagnosis</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8038-aa40-c7ee90592f67" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No intelligence ranking</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8022-a7d8-c30bcd3e70ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No identity labeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809a-b3bb-ef2282db7f37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No moral inference</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8094-be08-d1074ede940b" class="">It detects <strong>state dynamics</strong>, nothing else.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-8846-e77f5d17b327"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80db-8a80-fdeda904a400" class="">One precise synthesis sentence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a1-8f15-d63212f3da98" class="">A personalized HMM or switching linear dynamical system trained on multimodal autonomic, behavioral, cognitive, and timing features can reliably detect latent cognitive–physiological regimes, estimate transition hazards and dwell times, and identify reintegration failures without making diagnostic or normative claims.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e8-ae61-f3a2bb703a34"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809b-9199-c758d163f9d5" class="">If you want next, 
I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a9-9bbb-fe0495ca3a6d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">provide <strong>pseudo-code (PyTorch / Pyro / Stan)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8030-b55b-c71db6c94448" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">design a <strong>minimal pilot study</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-808b-aa8c-e236ef346237" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">map <strong>AI governance guardrails</strong> to avoid misuse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8079-bab7-f68384ef3f6f" class="">Below are <strong>reference pseudo-code templates</strong> for <strong>(1) Pyro (recommended for HMM/SLDS)</strong> and <strong>(2) Stan (HMM; SLDS in Stan is possible but heavy)</strong>. I’m keeping it <strong>implementable</strong>, but still “pseudo” (you’ll need to wire real data loading and choose K, dims, priors).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-808b-ab03-cd74472d7512"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b3-903b-e079a0f359f9" class="">1) Pyro: Gaussian HMM (multivariate emissions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/components/prism-python.min.js" integrity="sha512-AKaNmg8COK0zEbjTdMHJAPJ0z6VeNqvRvH4/d5M4sHJbQQUToMBtodq4HaV4fa+WV2UTfoperElm66c9/8cKmQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8053-a5c4-fa24131a0fed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># PSEUDO-CODE (Pyro) — Multivariate Gaussian HMM
# y_t: [T, D] feature vectors (already normalized per user)
# K: number of latent states

import torch
import pyro
import pyro.distributions as dist
from pyro.infer import SVI, Trace_ELBO
from pyro.optim import Adam

def model(y, K: int):
    T, D = y.shape

    # Priors for transition matrix
    with pyro.plate(&quot;rows&quot;, K):
        trans_probs = pyro.sample(&quot;trans_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(torch.ones(K)))

    # Prior for initial state distribution
    init_probs = pyro.sample(&quot;init_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(torch.ones(K)))

    # Emission parameters: state-specific mean and covariance
    # Use LKJ prior for correlation + HalfCauchy for scales for stability
    with pyro.plate(&quot;states&quot;, K):
        mu = pyro.sample(&quot;mu&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(D), 2.0).to_event(1))  # [K, D]

        # Diagonal covariance for MVP simplicity; swap to LKJ for full covariance
        sigma = pyro.sample(&quot;sigma&quot;, dist.HalfCauchy(scale=torch.ones(D)).to_event(1))  # [K, D]

    # Latent state sequence
    z_prev = pyro.sample(&quot;z_0&quot;, dist.Categorical(init_probs))

    for t in range(T):
        # emission
        pyro.sample(
            f&quot;y_{t}&quot;,
            dist.Normal(mu[z_prev], sigma[z_prev]).to_event(1),
            obs=y[t],
        )

        # transition
        z_prev = pyro.sample(f&quot;z_{t+1}&quot;, dist.Categorical(trans_probs[z_prev]))


def guide(y, K: int):
    # Variational params for transitions, init, emissions
    T, D = y.shape

    trans_q = pyro.param(&quot;trans_q&quot;, torch.ones(K, K), constraint=dist.constraints.positive)
    init_q  = pyro.param(&quot;init_q&quot;,  torch.ones(K),     constraint=dist.constraints.positive)

    mu_q    = pyro.param(&quot;mu_q&quot;,    torch.zeros(K, D))
    sigma_q = pyro.param(&quot;sigma_q&quot;, torch.ones(K, D),  constraint=dist.constraints.positive)

    with pyro.plate(&quot;rows&quot;, K):
        pyro.sample(&quot;trans_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(trans_q))

    pyro.sample(&quot;init_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(init_q))

    with pyro.plate(&quot;states&quot;, K):
        pyro.sample(&quot;mu&quot;, dist.Normal(mu_q, 0.1).to_event(1))
        pyro.sample(&quot;sigma&quot;, dist.LogNormal(torch.log(sigma_q), 0.1).to_event(1))

    # NOTE: This guide does not approximate z_t well.
    # For real use, replace with enumerated HMM inference or structured guide.
    # See Pyro&#x27;s HMM enumeration patterns.


# Training loop
def fit_hmm(y, K=6, steps=2000, lr=1e-2):
    optim = Adam({&quot;lr&quot;: lr})
    svi = SVI(lambda: model(y, K), lambda: guide(y, K), optim, loss=Trace_ELBO())

    for step in range(steps):
        loss = svi.step()
        if step % 100 == 0:
            print(step, loss)

    return pyro.get_param_store()

# Inference for z_t:
# In practice, use enumeration or run forward-backward to compute posterior p(z_t|y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c5-b1ad-df4a16d9f9e0" class=""><strong>Notes (important):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8048-b8b4-c8e5bcdac2a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">The above is a didactic template. For a <em>real</em> HMM, you should use <strong>enumeration</strong> or an explicit <strong>forward–backward</strong> routine to infer .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d6-a9de-f124e0f42fef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If you want <strong>full covariance</strong>, swap diagonal <code>sigma</code> for LKJ-cholesky covariance priors and use <code>MultivariateNormal</code>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-808a-bf9a-f8f98dc2cd6f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-805d-b0b7-c25b1a09336e" class="">2) Pyro: Switching Linear Dynamical System (SLDS) skeleton</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8014-b732-d9251df2f101" class="">This is the structure you want for C7-like phase detection.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8071-bd58-d5be70d9229e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-python" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># PSEUDO-CODE (Pyro) — SLDS (Switching Linear Dynamical System)
# x_t: latent continuous state (dim M)
# y_t: observed features (dim D)
# z_t: discrete regime (K states)
#
# x_{t+1} = A[z_t] x_t + b[z_t] + w_t,  w_t ~ N(0, Q[z_t])
# y_t     = C[z_t] x_t + d[z_t] + v_t,  v_t ~ N(0, R[z_t])

import torch
import pyro
import pyro.distributions as dist

def slds_model(y, K=6, M=8):
    T, D = y.shape

    # initial discrete + continuous priors
    init_probs = pyro.sample(&quot;init_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(torch.ones(K)))
    z_prev = pyro.sample(&quot;z_0&quot;, dist.Categorical(init_probs))

    x_prev = pyro.sample(&quot;x_0&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(M), 1.0).to_event(1))

    # transition matrix
    with pyro.plate(&quot;rows&quot;, K):
        trans_probs = pyro.sample(&quot;trans_probs&quot;, dist.Dirichlet(torch.ones(K)))

    # state-specific dynamics
    with pyro.plate(&quot;states&quot;, K):
        A = pyro.sample(&quot;A&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(M, M), 0.2).to_event(2))
        b = pyro.sample(&quot;b&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(M), 0.2).to_event(1))

        C = pyro.sample(&quot;C&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(D, M), 0.2).to_event(2))
        d = pyro.sample(&quot;d&quot;, dist.Normal(torch.zeros(D), 0.2).to_event(1))

        # diagonal process/obs noise for simplicity
        Q_diag = pyro.sample(&quot;Q_diag&quot;, dist.HalfCauchy(torch.ones(M)).to_event(1))
        R_diag = pyro.sample(&quot;R_diag&quot;, dist.HalfCauchy(torch.ones(D)).to_event(1))

    for t in range(T):
        # emission
        y_mean = C[z_prev] @ x_prev + d[z_prev]
        pyro.sample(
            f&quot;y_{t}&quot;,
            dist.Normal(y_mean, R_diag[z_prev]).to_event(1),
            obs=y[t],
        )

        # continuous transition
        x_mean = A[z_prev] @ x_prev + b[z_prev]
        x_prev = pyro.sample(
            f&quot;x_{t+1}&quot;,
            dist.Normal(x_mean, Q_diag[z_prev]).to_event(1)
        )

        # discrete transition
        z_prev = pyro.sample(
            f&quot;z_{t+1}&quot;,
            dist.Categorical(trans_probs[z_prev])
        )

def slds_guide(y, K=6, M=8):
    # For practical SLDS, use structured variational inference:
    # - amortized inference network for x_t and z_t
    # - or particle-based methods
    #
    # Here is only a skeleton stub.

    pass

# Practical recommendation:
# Use pyro.contrib or a library (ssm / dynamax / pyro-hmm patterns) for working SLDS inference.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8010-9130-e040f43490ec" class=""><strong>Practical note:</strong> SLDS inference is nontrivial.
