In production you typically use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8090-bc0c-ff8e61d8d045" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>structured VI</strong> (amortized inference network),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801b-8c78-d57bc2cf7ed4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>particle MCMC / SMC</strong>, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c0-9146-c22535bd60cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">an established library (e.g., <code>ssm</code>, <code>dynamax</code>) and wrap your features.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-805b-bd0e-c4cf08d51e97"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ce-9bd2-d924e5fb41d8" class="">3) Stan: Multinomial-logit HMM with Gaussian emissions (diagonal cov)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8085-b4fa-d256c83acfee" class="">Stan is excellent for HMMs with marginalization (forward algorithm). Here’s a clear template.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fe-bf1c-dec9a05d7514" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">// PSEUDO-CODE (Stan) — Gaussian HMM with forward algorithm
data {
  int&lt;lower=1&gt; T;
  int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;
  int&lt;lower=1&gt; K;
  matrix[T, D] y;  // normalized feature vectors
}

parameters {
  simplex[K] init_probs;
  simplex[K] trans_probs[K];

  matrix[K, D] mu;
  matrix&lt;lower=0&gt;[K, D] sigma; // diagonal std dev
}

model {
  // Priors (lightly informative)
  to_vector(mu) ~ normal(0, 2);
  to_vector(sigma) ~ cauchy(0, 1);

  // Forward algorithm (log space)
  vector[K] log_alpha[T];

  // t=1
  for (k in 1:K) {
    log_alpha[1, k] =
      log(init_probs[k]) +
      normal_lpdf(y[1] | mu[k], sigma[k]);
  }

  // t=2..T
  for (t in 2:T) {
    for (k in 1:K) {
      vector[K] tmp;
      for (j in 1:K) {
        tmp[j] = log_alpha[t-1, j] + log(trans_probs[j, k]);
      }
      log_alpha[t, k] =
        log_sum_exp(tmp) +
        normal_lpdf(y[t] | mu[k], sigma[k]);
    }
  }

  target += log_sum_exp(log_alpha[T]);
}

generated quantities {
  // You can implement backward pass for smoothed posteriors if needed.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-9feb-e22fdc314539" class=""><strong>Notes:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8050-bab3-e51fd65e2b43" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">This marginalizes discrete states; 
sampling is stable.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8067-ab07-f7145e2e823b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">For state posteriors , add a backward pass in <code>generated quantities</code> (or postprocess externally).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8050-ac84-f1243e0ace6d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8056-b66b-df63730280a7" class="">Recommended next step (so this becomes real)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808e-b0eb-dadbe7f8fc73" class="">Tell me which you want:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8013-a29b-d14f02ecef6b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Concrete forward–backward posterior code</strong> for HMM (Python) + how to compute dwell times and hazard of exit</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8022-b37f-f220da506628" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>A realistic SLDS implementation path</strong> using a known library (e.g., dynamax/ssm) + how to validate phase labels</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f4-a6bd-c85c5f3e2c75" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>A minimal end-to-end “per-user baseline → fit → infer → dashboards” scaffold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ff-bcff-d7d8cc5e26cf" class="">I’ll output code-level pseudocode (still no tool calls needed) accordingly.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-801c-a493-e0fe8f16cd02" class="">Below are <strong>three concrete deliverables</strong> (all in English, 
code-first):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e4-81ca-fad0efde7499" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Forward–backward posterior code for a Gaussian HMM</strong> (Python) + <strong>dwell times</strong> + <strong>hazard-of-exit</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b3-997e-f93f5c775160" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>A <strong>realistic SLDS implementation path</strong> using <strong>dynamax</strong> or <strong>ssm</strong> + <strong>phase-label validation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8052-a022-f0a7df177286" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>A <strong>minimal end-to-end scaffold</strong>: baseline → features → fit → infer → dashboards</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e0-9257-ee0ad2689107"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-806a-b7e5-dcfce3346587" class=""><strong>1) Concrete forward–backward posterior code (Python)</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f6-93e8-c4d90aa919ab" class=""><strong>1.1 Gaussian HMM (diagonal covariance) — log-space forward/backward</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fa-8195-ffff509c85c1" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Tuple

EPS = 1e-12

def logsumexp(a: np.ndarray, axis=None, keepdims=False) -&gt; np.ndarray:
    a_max = np.max(a, axis=axis, keepdims=True)
    out = a_max + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(a - a_max), axis=axis, keepdims=True) + EPS)
    if not keepdims:
        out = np.squeeze(out, axis=axis)
    return out

def log_normal_diag(y: np.ndarray, mu: np.ndarray, sigma: np.ndarray) -&gt; np.ndarray:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    y: [T, D]
    mu: [K, D]
    sigma: [K, D]  (std dev &gt; 0)
    returns loglik: [T, K]
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    T, D = y.shape
    K = mu.shape[0]
    y_exp = y[:, None, :]          # [T, 1, D]
    mu_exp = mu[None, :, :]        # [1, K, D]
    sig_exp = sigma[None, :, :]    # [1, K, D]

    z = (y_exp - mu_exp) / (sig_exp + EPS)
    log_det = np.sum(np.log(sig_exp + EPS), axis=-1)   # [1, K]
    quad = 0.5 * np.sum(z * z, axis=-1)                # [T, K]
    const = 0.5 * D * np.log(2.0 * np.pi)
    return -(const + log_det + quad)                   # [T, K]

@dataclass
class HMMParams:
    init: np.ndarray   # [K]
    trans: np.ndarray  # [K, K]  trans[i,j] = P(z_t=j | z_{t-1}=i)
    mu: np.ndarray     # [K, D]
    sigma: np.ndarray  # [K, D]

@dataclass
class HMMPosteriors:
    gamma: np.ndarray   # [T, K]  p(z_t=k | y)
    xi: np.ndarray      # [T-1, K, K]  p(z_t=i, z_{t+1}=j | y)
    loglik: float

def forward_backward(y: np.ndarray, params: HMMParams) -&gt; HMMPosteriors:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Computes smoothed posteriors via log-space forward-backward.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    init = np.clip(params.init, EPS, 1.0)
    trans = np.clip(params.trans, EPS, 1.0)
    init = init / init.sum()
    trans = trans / trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)

    T, D = y.shape
    K = init.shape[0]

    log_init = np.log(init)
    log_trans = np.log(trans)
    log_emiss = log_normal_diag(y, params.mu, params.sigma)  # [T, K]

    # Forward: log_alpha[t,k] = log p(y_0..y_t, z_t=k)
    log_alpha = np.zeros((T, K))
    log_alpha[0] = log_init + log_emiss[0]
    for t in range(1, T):
        # log_alpha[t,k] = log_emiss[t,k] + logsum_j log_alpha[t-1,j] + log_trans[j,k]
        tmp = log_alpha[t-1][:, None] + log_trans  # [K, K]
        log_alpha[t] = log_emiss[t] + logsumexp(tmp, axis=0)

    loglik = float(logsumexp(log_alpha[T-1], axis=0))

    # Backward: log_beta[t,k] = log p(y_{t+1}..y_{T-1} | z_t=k)
    log_beta = np.zeros((T, K))
    log_beta[T-1] = 0.0
    for t in range(T-2, -1, -1):
        # log_beta[t,i] = logsum_j log_trans[i,j] + log_emiss[t+1,j] + log_beta[t+1,j]
        tmp = log_trans + (log_emiss[t+1] + log_beta[t+1])[None, :]  # [K, K]
        log_beta[t] = logsumexp(tmp, axis=1)

    # gamma[t,k] ∝ exp(log_alpha[t,k] + log_beta[t,k])
    log_gamma = log_alpha + log_beta
    log_gamma = log_gamma - logsumexp(log_gamma, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    gamma = np.exp(log_gamma)  # [T, K]

    # xi[t,i,j] ∝ alpha[t,i] * trans[i,j] * emiss[t+1,j] * beta[t+1,j]
    xi = np.zeros((T-1, K, K))
    for t in range(T-1):
        log_xi_t = (
            log_alpha[t][:, None]
            + log_trans
            + log_emiss[t+1][None, :]
            + log_beta[t+1][None, :]
        )  # [K, K]
        log_xi_t = log_xi_t - logsumexp(log_xi_t, axis=(0, 1))
        xi[t] = np.exp(log_xi_t)

    return HMMPosteriors(gamma=gamma, xi=xi, loglik=loglik)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80be-b8df-c033fa6d6cea" class=""><strong>1.2 Dwell times + hazard-of-exit (state persistence / “stuck” detection)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80fd-af08-fd723dd180f3" class=""><strong>Two ways</strong> to estimate “hazard of exit”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-805e-8b92-e964cebcdb9e" class=""><strong>(A) Markov hazard from transition matrix (model-based, simple)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e9-9854-cbec33cd67b8" class="">For state k, per-step exit probability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80db-8c92-d884b66d6a73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">h_k = 1 - P(z_{t+1}=k \mid z_t=k) = 1 - A_{kk}</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8063-acc5-c619a93af320" class="">Expected dwell length (geometric):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c9-ac15-ca92235dd2d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">E[L_k] = 1 / (1 - A_{kk})</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-80c2-d1cec925bd09" class=""><strong>(B) Empirical hazard conditioned on dwell duration (data-driven)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8005-9fcc-f0956415a011" class="">Compute survival curve of runs in each state from a decoded or posterior sample.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a5-9496-e05f8e5f51be" class="">Below code provides both, 
plus posterior-based expected dwell time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ea-a44a-d993589e8c5f" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">from typing import Dict, List

def hazard_from_trans(trans: np.ndarray) -&gt; Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    trans: [K,K]
    Returns:
      hazard[k] = 1 - trans[k,k]
      expected_dwell[k] = 1 / hazard[k]
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    K = trans.shape[0]
    hazard = 1.0 - np.clip(np.diag(trans), 0.0, 1.0)
    hazard = np.maximum(hazard, EPS)
    expected_dwell = 1.0 / hazard
    return hazard, expected_dwell

def viterbi_decode(y: np.ndarray, params: HMMParams) -&gt; np.ndarray:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Simple Viterbi decode for most likely state sequence.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    init = np.clip(params.init, EPS, 1.0)
    trans = np.clip(params.trans, EPS, 1.0)
    init = init / init.sum()
    trans = trans / trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)

    T, D = y.shape
    K = init.shape[0]
    log_init = np.log(init)
    log_trans = np.log(trans)
    log_emiss = log_normal_diag(y, params.mu, params.sigma)  # [T,K]

    dp = np.zeros((T, K))
    ptr = np.zeros((T, K), dtype=int)

    dp[0] = log_init + log_emiss[0]
    for t in range(1, T):
        scores = dp[t-1][:, None] + log_trans  # [K,K]
        ptr[t] = np.argmax(scores, axis=0)
        dp[t] = log_emiss[t] + np.max(scores, axis=0)

    z = np.zeros(T, dtype=int)
    z[T-1] = int(np.argmax(dp[T-1]))
    for t in range(T-2, -1, -1):
        z[t] = ptr[t+1, z[t+1]]
    return z

def run_lengths(z: np.ndarray) -&gt; Dict[int, List[int]]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Returns list of run lengths for each state.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    runs: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}
    if len(z) == 0:
        return runs
    curr = z[0]
    length = 1
    for t in range(1, len(z)):
        if z[t] == curr:
            length += 1
        else:
            runs.setdefault(int(curr), []).append(length)
            curr = z[t]
            length = 1
    runs.setdefault(int(curr), []).append(length)
    return runs

def empirical_hazard_by_duration(runs: Dict[int, List[int]], max_d: int = 30) -&gt; Dict[int, np.ndarray]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Empirical discrete-time hazard h(d) = P(exit at d | survived to d)
    where d = 1..max_d (run length in steps).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    hazards = {}
    for k, lengths in runs.items():
        lengths = np.array(lengths, dtype=int)
        # survival count at duration d: number of runs with length &gt;= d
        surv = np.array([(lengths &gt;= d).sum() for d in range(1, max_d+1)], dtype=float)
        # exit count at duration d: number of runs with length == d
        exitc = np.array([(lengths == d).sum() for d in range(1, max_d+1)], dtype=float)
        h = np.where(surv &gt; 0, exitc / surv, 0.0)
        hazards[k] = h
    return hazards

def posterior_expected_dwell(gamma: np.ndarray) -&gt; np.ndarray:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Rough posterior expected dwell proxy:
    expected consecutive occupancy can be approximated by state self-transition
    estimated from xi / gamma if you have xi.
    This function just returns mean occupancy; use xi-based estimate for transitions.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    return gamma.mean(axis=0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-800e-8226-ccb032d3d1fc" class=""><strong>How to use for “stuck” alerts</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8094-8572-fb35e8b83536" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Model-based: flag if A_{kk} is very high (e.g., &gt; 0.95 daily step) → expected dwell &gt; 
20 days</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8071-bb33-f0aa9b7fe15c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Empirical: hazard curve h_k(d) drops toward 0 as d increases → increasing persistence → “stuck” signature</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-8003-b8a6-c833993cb4a4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8026-887d-ce72d7ca248c" class=""><strong>2) Realistic SLDS implementation path (dynamax / ssm) + phase-label validation</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-802b-b6b4-fa2ece87414b" class=""><strong>2.1 Recommended path:</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8086-94ad-d1bdbb5ad6b1" class=""><strong>dynamax</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80de-bade-f64a51ec7f10" class=""><strong>(JAX) for SLDS</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809a-a3a5-c3a408fa848c" class=""><strong>Why:</strong> cleaner SLDS primitives, modern inference, stable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f6-b522-ca2f7f8c3e69" class=""><strong>Plan</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8027-ae8f-eab09b3a12bb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Build <strong>feature vectors</strong> y_t \in \mathbb{R}^D per day (or per hour)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cf-8a4a-df1cff2e295e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Choose latent continuous dimension M (e.g., 6–12)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-804c-953f-f885070a18b3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Fit SLDS with K regimes (5–8), 
diagonal or low-rank covariances</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-a471-e75ef8209f2d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Infer:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-a70f-de6ac5844fe7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">p(z_t|y) regime posteriors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8041-b1a9-dd5b5c783622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">x_t latent trajectories</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8098-a860-d69425359e52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">dwell times / transition hazards</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-801a-813c-f22c93864e7d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Map regimes → phase labels <strong>post hoc</strong> (don’t bake labels into training)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-805f-ba7a-fad37092d09e" class=""><strong>Pseudo-implementation outline (conceptual)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e5-9368-e6ce34d41bc4" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># PSEUDO (conceptual) - actual API details differ by dynamax version
# from dynamax.hidden_markov_model import ...
# from dynamax.linear_gaussian_ssm import ...
# from dynamax.slds import ...

# 1) Prepare y: [T, D] per user, normalized
# 2) Initialize params (K regimes, M latent dim)
# 3) Fit with EM or variational inference
# 4) Compute posterior regime probs: q_z[t,k]
# 5) Export phase timeline + hazards</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bc-bd80-fa34378407a5" class="">If you use dynamax, treat it like:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801b-84be-f60eb810ae5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“HMM over regimes”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8019-a8fb-e111da54d627" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">each regime has its own LDS (A_k, Q_k, C_k, R_k)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b1-bff6-e6cdb032ccff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8091-91f5-fdeeb7319713" class=""><strong>2.2 Alternative:</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cd-a7d0-cc2a2291d502" class=""><strong>ssm</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-804b-8545-fb01484b650e" class=""><strong>(Python) for SLDS-like models</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ef-b4c8-c112a9867a9a" class=""><strong>Why:</strong> pragmatic; 
many use it for switching LDS.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808e-81a2-e7d1138756dc" class=""><strong>Plan</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8068-9d15-e9e6e1d9aaab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Use ssm.SLDS or ssm.LDS with switching</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805a-9919-d4e7f444d7d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fit with EM</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-807e-8438-eec7d407e4bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Use posterior expected_states for regime probabilities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8032-9328-f92d97bf9a29" class=""><strong>Conceptual outline</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8073-942f-ce4d4bde6808" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># pip install ssm (note: sometimes requires older autograd/numpy setup)

import ssm
import numpy as np

T, D = y.shape
K = 6
M = 8

slds = ssm.SLDS(
    N=T, K=K, D=D, latent_dim=M,
    emissions=&quot;gaussian&quot;, dynamics=&quot;gaussian&quot;
)

# Fit (EM)
elbos = slds.fit(y, method=&quot;laplace_em&quot;, num_iters=200)

# Posterior regime probabilities
qz = slds.expected_states(y)[0]   # often returns (Ez, Ezzp1, ...)
# qz: [T, K]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ba-8de0-f3c6333b93a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ec-8263-d19d0c746f9f" class=""><strong>2.3 Validation: how to label regimes as “C7-like” without overclaiming</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80cb-8393-fa5682937f7b" class=""><strong>Do NOT validate on “insight feelings” alone.</strong> Use orthogonal signals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f8-87dc-fd374a5bce90" class=""><strong>Validation targets (strongest → weakest)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f6-a498-c5099af14a36" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Sleep-based integration:</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80a1-a7b9-cee90495bf4a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">improvements in sleep efficiency / REM/SWS continuity precede transitions into C7-like regimes</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-800a-9c08-f83343b6a9b1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Performance rebound after rest:</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8041-abe6-d204391c6395" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">microtask gains post-sleep correlate with C7-like posterior</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-8032-8f94-c41e2ab58f22" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Reduced behavioral fragmentation:</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8029-8c13-f4b0cf926687" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fewer context switches, longer sessions (not necessarily “more work”, 
but more continuity)</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ac-beee-cbc68201427c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Autonomic stability:</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8023-b4fa-f1d7ffe63b61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HRV increases + faster recovery slopes</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8056-beda-d4d3fd64ff77" class=""><strong>Labeling rule (example)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80de-92ce-c6111dce1965" class="">Call a regime “C7-like” if, relative to that user’s baseline:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ad-904c-d57568015107" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HRV ↑ (sustained), fragmentation ↓, sleep regularity ↑, post-rest gain ↑<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-808e-a5e1-c1909b37c347" class="">and the regime has:</p></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-82f8-f175c8dfbceb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">moderate dwell time (bounded), 
not endless persistence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-809b-af1a-ff694cfdb839" class=""><strong>Cross-validation design</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ee-becb-d00061c0c094" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Train regimes on weeks 1–N</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8042-9b1f-ec398b00b852" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Validate label mapping on weeks N+1–N+M using held-out metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80df-a2ad-e23644d4bd88"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8045-a82e-e30b51d7d4bf" class=""><strong>3) Minimal end-to-end scaffold (baseline → fit → infer → dashboards)</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8072-8f38-fde530e009c7" class=""><strong>3.1 Repository structure</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bc-aa7f-c11aecd7bc0d" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">phase-model/
  README.md
  pyproject.toml
  data/
    raw/
    processed/
  src/
    config.py
    ingest/
      wearable.py
      device_events.py
    features/
      build_features.py
      normalize.py
    models/
      hmm.py
      slds_wrapper.py
      inference.py
    validation/
      label_mapping.py
      metrics.py
    dashboards/
      app_streamlit.py
      plots.py
  notebooks/
    01_baseline.ipynb
    02_fit_hmm.ipynb
    03_fit_slds.ipynb</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8081-bb7f-ec1d2cad3207" class=""><strong>3.2 Baseline + feature build (per user)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8022-9c9d-c4000870a583" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># src/features/normalize.py
import numpy as np

def rolling_zscore(x: np.ndarray, window: int = 28) -&gt; np.ndarray:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    x: [T, D] daily features
    returns z-scored by rolling window baseline.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    T, D = x.shape
    z = np.zeros_like(x)
    for t in range(T):
        s = max(0, t - window)
        base = x[s:t+1]
        mu = base.mean(axis=0)
        sd = base.std(axis=0) + 1e-6
        z[t] = (x[t] - mu) / sd
    return z</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8097-b3ac-f7fbb1c89361" class=""><strong>3.3 Fit HMM (simple EM) + infer posteriors</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-800a-b760-feeb5371e369" class="">You can either:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8096-bfe4-e3c558915d3f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">start with fixed params (k-means init), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-bcdd-c2aae0bb8b09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">implement EM updates.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-807e-9569-f9a81763c803" class="">Below is a <strong>minimal EM loop</strong> for diagonal Gaussian HMM:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-801c-a87f-c2a0fdbf0861" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># src/models/hmm.py
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple
from .fb import forward_backward, HMMParams  # assume you placed code from section 1 in fb.py

def em_fit_hmm(y: np.ndarray, K: int, iters: int = 50, seed: int = 0) -&gt; HMMParams:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    EM for Gaussian HMM with diagonal covariance.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    T, D = y.shape

    # init with random responsibilities
    resp = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=T)  # [T,K]

    init = resp[0] / resp[0].sum()
    trans = np.ones((K, K)) / K

    mu = (resp.T @ y) / (resp.sum(axis=0)[:, None] + 1e-9)  # [K,D]
    var = np.zeros((K, D))
    for k in range(K):
        diff = y - mu[k]
        var[k] = (resp[:, k][:, None] * diff * diff).sum(axis=0) / (resp[:, k].sum() + 1e-9)
    sigma = np.sqrt(var + 1e-6)

    params = HMMParams(init=init, trans=trans, mu=mu, sigma=sigma)

    for _ in range(iters):
        post = forward_backward(y, params)
        gamma, xi = post.gamma, post.xi

        # M-step
        init = gamma[0]
        trans = xi.sum(axis=0)  # [K,K]
        trans = trans / (trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)

        mu = (gamma.T @ y) / (gamma.sum(axis=0)[:, None] + 1e-12)
        var = np.zeros((K, D))
        for k in range(K):
            diff = y - mu[k]
            var[k] = (gamma[:, k][:, None] * diff * diff).sum(axis=0) / (gamma[:, k].sum() + 1e-12)
        sigma = np.sqrt(var + 1e-6)

        params = HMMParams(init=init, trans=trans, mu=mu, sigma=sigma)

    return params</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80da-833f-e86233502860" class=""><strong>3.4 Dashboard (Streamlit + Plotly)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d3-9505-fae25c613454" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all"># src/dashboards/app_streamlit.py
import streamlit as st
import plotly.express as px
import numpy as np

def main():
    st.title(&quot;Phase Timeline (HMM/SLDS)&quot;)

    # Load artifacts you saved:
    # gamma: [T,K], dates: [T], features: [T,D]
    gamma = np.load(&quot;data/processed/gamma.npy&quot;)
    dates = np.load(&quot;data/processed/dates.npy&quot;, allow_pickle=True)

    # Plot state probabilities
    df = {&quot;date&quot;: dates}
    K = gamma.shape[1]
    for k in range(K):
        df[f&quot;state_{k}&quot;] = gamma[:, k]
    import pandas as pd
    df = pd.DataFrame(df)

    fig = px.line(df, x=&quot;date&quot;, y=[f&quot;state_{k}&quot; for k in range(K)])
    st.plotly_chart(fig, use_container_width=True)

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    main()</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b9-b59c-fce4f6085dc7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8088-a6cb-c983a3088c2b" class=""><strong>What I need from you to make this fully “drop-in”</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b1-8379-d87efbee5d47" class="">Pick one:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-805e-b5a0-e02c3cc7f2af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Daily</strong> timestep (simpler, recommended first), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8096-a85f-cb74b7ab715d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Hourly</strong> timestep (better transitions, more data cleaning)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8080-b925-c5a6e50ca118" class="">And confirm:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ac-b611-dd0d8ca5f12d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">your feature matrix shape convention (T×D) and whether you already have D≈30.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b4-84ee-c9791a8a8e50" class="">If you don’t want to answer, default is: <strong>daily, T×30</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="303c5e6f-95bd-80bf-ad62-caadf8653d79"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="303c5e6f-95bd-801e-91df-f56890d2ff98" class=""><strong>Next step options (no extra questions required)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8048-948b-d22c934f7de3" class="">If you want, 
I can output <strong>one integrated Python module</strong> that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f4-a697-dae58cae3f24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">loads y.npy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-80f0-a89a-d38080e30c78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fits EM-HMM</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-802d-bfd5-e03fbd914b15" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">runs forward–backward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-808f-89d8-fc5a0a31637d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">computes hazards + dwell alerts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8097-9930-ea357d6e9d2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exports gamma.npy + plots</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8074-8d50-d943465569a0" class="">Just say: <strong>“Generate integrated module.”</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-806a-aaf8-cf60ea546577" class="">&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>phase_hmm_pipeline.py<br/>End-to-end per-user pipeline:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8008-ac2f-e5f48c7cb9a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Load y.npy (T x D) + optional dates.npy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8003-a4a8-ec6b73bac4f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Normalize (rolling z-score)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8045-8ac4-c3a487d96919" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fit Gaussian HMM (diagonal cov) via EM</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-808a-a18f-deb304353764" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Forward-backward posteriors (gamma, xi)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-8084-9b8c-e1c31740c4c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compute dwell/hazard (model + empirical)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="303c5e6f-95bd-809a-be85-dd4fd826b32e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Export artifacts + generate plots</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8096-8cce-d6061f2a21a3" class="">USAGE</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8077-9fb4-cf0bc24475c7" class="">python phase_hmm_pipeline.py --input_y data/processed/y.npy --out_dir data/processed --K 6 --iters 60</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8009-8cd9-ebcd510d3adf" class="">Optional:<br/>--input_dates data/processed/dates.npy<br/>--window 28<br/>--step daily   (just metadata)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e4-9e21-e1ef372b9ca2" class="">Outputs</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8023-a525-c26b68719cb9" class="">out_dir/<br/>hmm_params.npz<br/>gamma.npy<br/>xi.npy<br/>loglik.txt<br/>hazards_model.npy<br/>expected_dwell_model.npy<br/>viterbi_states.npy<br/>empirical_hazard.json<br/>plots/<br/>gamma_probs.png<br/>viterbi_timeline.png<br/>dwell_histograms.png<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80ba-8df7-f7e1f2a2e7dc" class="">from <strong>future</strong> import annotations</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8059-868c-c57833dc829a" class="">import argparse<br/>import json<br/>import os<br/>from dataclasses import dataclass<br/>from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, 
Optional</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b0-8f1e-cf267127b564" class="">import numpy as np</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8061-8ff3-cb5d54e28aa8" class="">EPS = 1e-12</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b5-b6d9-eb1a962ac1ba" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-805d-81c7-d82c48a6fa97" class="">Utilities</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8058-b323-c1e33f6fc566" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8074-b5d1-d1de2b9e1843" class="">def ensure_dir(path: str) -&gt; None:<br/>os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8015-bf93-c993c43e64c5" class="">def logsumexp(a: np.ndarray, axis=None, keepdims: bool = False) -&gt; np.ndarray:<br/>a_max = np.max(a, axis=axis, keepdims=True)<br/>out = a_max + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(a - a_max), axis=axis, keepdims=True) + EPS)<br/>if not keepdims and axis is not None:<br/>out = np.squeeze(out, axis=axis)<br/>return out</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8075-ac1f-f5c8352aacb0" class="">def rolling_zscore(x: np.ndarray, window: int = 28) -&gt; 
np.ndarray:<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>Rolling z-score normalization per feature.<br/>x: [T, D]<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>T, D = x.shape<br/>z = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=float)<br/>for t in range(T):<br/>s = max(0, t - window)<br/>base = x[s : t + 1]<br/>mu = base.mean(axis=0)<br/>sd = base.std(axis=0) + 1e-6<br/>z[t] = (x[t] - mu) / sd<br/>return z</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8075-a5a3-e9ac96e36485" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d1-a597-d48955238144" class="">HMM core: emissions + FB</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80db-a2c3-db2b036c4fdc" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c8-9f35-f641d6ba8099" class="">def log_normal_diag(y: np.ndarray, mu: np.ndarray, sigma: np.ndarray) -&gt; np.ndarray:<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>y: [T, D]<br/>mu: [K, D]<br/>sigma: [K, D] (std dev)<br/>returns loglik: [T, K]<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>T, D = y.shape<br/>K = mu.shape[0]</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8056-896b-fce1a1e6a3e6" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">y_exp = y[:, None, :]        # [T,1,D]
mu_exp = mu[None, :, :]      # [1,K,D]
sig_exp = sigma[None, :, :]  # [1,K,D]

z = (y_exp - mu_exp) / (sig_exp + EPS)
log_det = np.sum(np.log(sig_exp + EPS), axis=-1)  # [1,K]
quad = 0.5 * np.sum(z * z, axis=-1)               # [T,K]
const = 0.5 * D * np.log(2.0 * np.pi)
return -(const + log_det + quad)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8005-b0c0-cf04e6a031c4" class="">@dataclass<br/>class HMMParams:<br/>init: np.ndarray   # [K]<br/>trans: np.ndarray  # [K,K]<br/>mu: np.ndarray     # [K,D]<br/>sigma: np.ndarray  # [K,D]</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b2-b346-cf77b7c3556b" class="">@dataclass<br/>class HMMPosteriors:<br/>gamma: np.ndarray   # [T,K]<br/>xi: np.ndarray      # [T-1,K,K]<br/>loglik: float</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-806e-9730-eeb026fd2746" class="">def forward_backward(y: np.ndarray, params: HMMParams) -&gt; HMMPosteriors:<br/>init = np.clip(params.init, EPS, 1.0)<br/>trans = np.clip(params.trans, EPS, 1.0)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-804c-8fbc-e242b5e3a550" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">init = init / init.sum()
trans = trans / (trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True) + EPS)

T, D = y.shape
K = init.shape[0]

log_init = np.log(init)
log_trans = np.log(trans)
log_emiss = log_normal_diag(y, params.mu, params.sigma)  # [T,K]

# Forward
log_alpha = np.zeros((T, K), dtype=float)
log_alpha[0] = log_init + log_emiss[0]
for t in range(1, T):
    tmp = log_alpha[t - 1][:, None] + log_trans  # [K,K]
    log_alpha[t] = log_emiss[t] + logsumexp(tmp, axis=0)

loglik = float(logsumexp(log_alpha[T - 1], axis=0))

# Backward
log_beta = np.zeros((T, K), dtype=float)
log_beta[T - 1] = 0.0
for t in range(T - 2, -1, -1):
    tmp = log_trans + (log_emiss[t + 1] + log_beta[t + 1])[None, :]  # [K,K]
    log_beta[t] = logsumexp(tmp, axis=1)

# Gamma
log_gamma = log_alpha + log_beta
log_gamma -= logsumexp(log_gamma, axis=1, keepdims=True)
gamma = np.exp(log_gamma)

# Xi
xi = np.zeros((T - 1, K, K), dtype=float)
for t in range(T - 1):
    log_xi_t = (
        log_alpha[t][:, None]
        + log_trans
        + log_emiss[t + 1][None, :]
        + log_beta[t + 1][None, :]
    )
    log_xi_t -= logsumexp(log_xi_t, axis=(0, 1))
    xi[t] = np.exp(log_xi_t)

return HMMPosteriors(gamma=gamma, xi=xi, loglik=loglik)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-806e-9aa1-e9ddccc26a09" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8060-acf8-d17f309120a4" class="">Viterbi decode + runs</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8098-88ac-e0e819bd0ff0" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8052-acc4-ff7a8457ff5b" class="">def viterbi_decode(y: np.ndarray, params: HMMParams) -&gt; np.ndarray:<br/>init = np.clip(params.init, EPS, 1.0)<br/>trans = np.clip(params.trans, EPS, 1.0)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80c9-af31-fa3623ecac17" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">init = init / init.sum()
trans = trans / (trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True) + EPS)

T, D = y.shape
K = init.shape[0]

log_init = np.log(init)
log_trans = np.log(trans)
log_emiss = log_normal_diag(y, params.mu, params.sigma)  # [T,K]

dp = np.zeros((T, K), dtype=float)
ptr = np.zeros((T, K), dtype=int)

dp[0] = log_init + log_emiss[0]
for t in range(1, T):
    scores = dp[t - 1][:, None] + log_trans  # [K,K]
    ptr[t] = np.argmax(scores, axis=0)
    dp[t] = log_emiss[t] + np.max(scores, axis=0)

z = np.zeros(T, dtype=int)
z[T - 1] = int(np.argmax(dp[T - 1]))
for t in range(T - 2, -1, -1):
    z[t] = ptr[t + 1, z[t + 1]]
return z</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8087-919e-ed547917c448" class="">def run_lengths(z: np.ndarray) -&gt; Dict[int, List[int]]:<br/>runs: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}<br/>if len(z) == 0:<br/>return runs<br/>curr = int(z[0])<br/>length = 1<br/>for t in range(1, len(z)):<br/>if int(z[t]) == curr:<br/>length += 1<br/>else:<br/>runs.setdefault(curr, []).append(length)<br/>curr = int(z[t])<br/>length = 1<br/>runs.setdefault(curr, []).append(length)<br/>return runs</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-80d7-a379-ecca2c632550" class="">def empirical_hazard_by_duration(runs: Dict[int, List[int]], max_d: int = 30) -&gt; Dict[int, List[float]]:<br/>hazards: Dict[int, List[float]] = {}<br/>for k, lengths in runs.items():<br/>arr = np.array(lengths, dtype=int)<br/>surv = np.array([(arr &gt;= d).sum() for d in range(1, max_d + 1)], dtype=float)<br/>exitc = np.array([(arr == d).sum() for d in range(1, max_d + 1)], dtype=float)<br/>h = np.where(surv &gt; 0, exitc / surv, 0.0)<br/>hazards[int(k)] = [float(v) for v in h]<br/>return hazards</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-803b-b265-ee12d1750e20" class="">def hazard_from_trans(trans: np.ndarray) -&gt; 
Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:<br/>K = trans.shape[0]<br/>self_p = np.clip(np.diag(trans), 0.0, 1.0)<br/>hazard = np.maximum(1.0 - self_p, EPS)<br/>expected_dwell = 1.0 / hazard<br/>return hazard, expected_dwell</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8017-a8a7-f9c09089c57e" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80b4-b072-effb69f6dec5" class="">EM training (Gaussian diag)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e6-93c3-e5a966e69d58" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8079-ae71-f31d5514d90c" class="">def init_params_kmeans_like(y: np.ndarray, K: int, seed: int = 0) -&gt; HMMParams:<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>Lightweight initialization:<br/>- random assignment -&gt; cluster means/vars<br/>- uniform transitions<br/>&quot;&quot;&quot;<br/>rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)<br/>T, D = y.shape<br/>resp = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K), size=T)  # soft assignments</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8031-8888-fb70fcbff236" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">init = resp[0] / (resp[0].sum() + EPS)
trans = np.ones((K, K), dtype=float) / K

mu = (resp.T @ y) / (resp.sum(axis=0)[:, None] + EPS)
var = np.zeros((K, D), dtype=float)
for k in range(K):
    diff = y - mu[k]
    var[k] = (resp[:, k][:, None] * diff * diff).sum(axis=0) / (resp[:, k].sum() + EPS)
sigma = np.sqrt(var + 1e-6)
return HMMParams(init=init, trans=trans, mu=mu, sigma=sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8041-944d-d209f415ad54" class="">def em_fit_hmm(y: np.ndarray, K: int, iters: int = 60, seed: int = 0, verbose: bool = True) -&gt; Tuple[HMMParams, List[float]]:<br/>params = init_params_kmeans_like(y, K=K, seed=seed)<br/>logliks: List[float] = []</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8004-9014-f2b997b4221b" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">for it in range(iters):
    post = forward_backward(y, params)
    gamma, xi = post.gamma, post.xi

    # M-step
    init = gamma[0] / (gamma[0].sum() + EPS)

    trans = xi.sum(axis=0)  # [K,K]
    trans = trans / (trans.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True) + EPS)

    mu = (gamma.T @ y) / (gamma.sum(axis=0)[:, None] + EPS)

    var = np.zeros_like(mu)
    for k in range(K):
        diff = y - mu[k]
        var[k] = (gamma[:, k][:, None] * diff * diff).sum(axis=0) / (gamma[:, k].sum() + EPS)
    sigma = np.sqrt(var + 1e-6)

    params = HMMParams(init=init, trans=trans, mu=mu, sigma=sigma)
    logliks.append(post.loglik)

    if verbose and (it % 10 == 0 or it == iters - 1):
        print(f&quot;[EM] iter={it:03d} loglik={post.loglik:.3f}&quot;)

return params, logliks</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8021-af76-d07dee9ca641" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8052-aee9-e527436ceac7" class="">Plotting (matplotlib only)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8085-8cdc-e715f3c5d2a9" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-806c-b828-f1496765504f" class="">def plot_gamma(gamma: np.ndarray, out_path: str, dates: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -&gt; None:<br/>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-803e-91e1-e453b7f08348" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">T, K = gamma.shape
x = np.arange(T) if dates is None else np.arange(T)

plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
for k in range(K):
    plt.plot(x, gamma[:, k], label=f&quot;state_{k}&quot;, linewidth=1.5)
plt.ylim(0, 1)
plt.xlabel(&quot;time&quot; if dates is None else &quot;index (dates provided)&quot;)
plt.ylabel(&quot;p(state | y)&quot;)
plt.title(&quot;HMM Smoothed State Probabilities (gamma)&quot;)
plt.legend(ncol=min(K, 6), fontsize=9)
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(out_path, dpi=160)
plt.close()</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8041-aaef-fa87449a78f2" class="">def plot_viterbi(z: np.ndarray, out_path: str, dates: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -&gt; None:<br/>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-80e0-ac12-cf18e555b959" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">T = len(z)
x = np.arange(T)
plt.figure(figsize=(12, 2.8))
plt.step(x, z, where=&quot;mid&quot;)
plt.yticks(sorted(set(z)))
plt.xlabel(&quot;time&quot; if dates is None else &quot;index (dates provided)&quot;)
plt.ylabel(&quot;state&quot;)
plt.title(&quot;Viterbi Most Likely State Sequence&quot;)
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(out_path, dpi=160)
plt.close()</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8007-bf5b-dfc22ffa2b48" class="">def plot_dwell_hist(runs: Dict[int, List[int]], out_path: str) -&gt; None:<br/>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-8099-b33d-fc2e011091ed" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">plt.figure(figsize=(12, 6))
for k, lengths in sorted(runs.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[0]):
    plt.hist(lengths, bins=range(1, max(lengths) + 2), alpha=0.5, label=f&quot;state_{k}&quot;)
plt.xlabel(&quot;run length (steps)&quot;)
plt.ylabel(&quot;count&quot;)
plt.title(&quot;Run Length (Dwell) Histograms by State (Viterbi)&quot;)
plt.legend(ncol=3, fontsize=9)
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(out_path, dpi=160)
plt.close()</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8077-9c35-d7a1421eb6c2" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-807d-8e15-e28e63920132" class="">Main</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="303c5e6f-95bd-8077-a2ce-feca38214e19" class="">----------------------------</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8044-bea1-e7bb6e82543d" class="">def main() -&gt; None:<br/>ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--input_y&quot;, required=True, help=&quot;Path to y.npy (T x D)&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--input_dates&quot;, default=None, help=&quot;Optional dates.npy (T,)&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--out_dir&quot;, required=True, help=&quot;Output directory&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--K&quot;, type=int, default=6, help=&quot;Number of HMM states&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--iters&quot;, type=int, default=60, help=&quot;EM iterations&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--seed&quot;, type=int, default=0, help=&quot;Random seed&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--window&quot;, type=int, default=28, help=&quot;Rolling z-score window&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--max_hazard_d&quot;, type=int, default=30, help=&quot;Max duration for empirical hazard curve&quot;)<br/>ap.add_argument(&quot;--no_norm&quot;, action=&quot;store_true&quot;, help=&quot;Disable rolling z-score normalization&quot;)<br/>args = ap.parse_args()</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="303c5e6f-95bd-801a-a76e-ca7785bf93e0" class="code code-wrap"><code style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">ensure_dir(args.out_dir)
plots_dir = os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;plots&quot;)
ensure_dir(plots_dir)

y = np.load(args.input_y).astype(float)
if y.ndim != 2:
    raise ValueError(&quot;y.npy must be 2D array with shape [T, D]&quot;)

dates = None
if args.input_dates:
    dates = np.load(args.input_dates, allow_pickle=True)

# Normalize
y_proc = y if args.no_norm else rolling_zscore(y, window=args.window)

# Fit HMM
params, logliks = em_fit_hmm(y_proc, K=args.K, iters=args.iters, seed=args.seed, verbose=True)

# Infer posteriors
post = forward_backward(y_proc, params)
gamma, xi, loglik = post.gamma, post.xi, post.loglik

# Dwell/hazard
hazard_model, expected_dwell_model = hazard_from_trans(params.trans)

z_vit = viterbi_decode(y_proc, params)
runs = run_lengths(z_vit)
hazards_emp = empirical_hazard_by_duration(runs, max_d=args.max_hazard_d)

# Save artifacts
np.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;gamma.npy&quot;), gamma)
np.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;xi.npy&quot;), xi)
np.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;viterbi_states.npy&quot;), z_vit)

np.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;hazards_model.npy&quot;), hazard_model)
np.save(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;expected_dwell_model.npy&quot;), expected_dwell_model)

np.savez(
    os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;hmm_params.npz&quot;),
    init=params.init,
    trans=params.trans,
    mu=params.mu,
    sigma=params.sigma,
    logliks=np.array(logliks, dtype=float),
)

with open(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;loglik.txt&quot;), &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
    f.write(f&quot;loglik_final\\t{loglik}\\n&quot;)
    f.write(f&quot;logliks\\t{&#x27;,&#x27;.join([f&#x27;{v:.6f}&#x27; for v in logliks])}\\n&quot;)

with open(os.path.join(args.out_dir, &quot;empirical_hazard.json&quot;), &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
    json.dump(hazards_emp, f, indent=2)

# Plots
plot_gamma(gamma, os.path.join(plots_dir, &quot;gamma_probs.png&quot;), dates=dates)
plot_viterbi(z_vit, os.path.join(plots_dir, &quot;viterbi_timeline.png&quot;), dates=dates)
plot_dwell_hist(runs, os.path.join(plots_dir, &quot;dwell_histograms.png&quot;))

# Console summary
print(&quot;\\n=== SUMMARY ===&quot;)
print(f&quot;T={y_proc.shape[0]} D={y_proc.shape[1]} K={args.K}&quot;)
print(f&quot;Final log-likelihood: {loglik:.3f}&quot;)
print(&quot;Model hazard per step (1 - A_kk):&quot;, np.round(hazard_model, 4))
print(&quot;Expected dwell (steps):&quot;, np.round(expected_dwell_model, 2))
print(&quot;Run lengths (Viterbi) per state:&quot;, {k: runs[k] for k in sorted(runs)})

print(f&quot;\\nSaved to: {args.out_dir}&quot;)
print(f&quot;Plots: {plots_dir}&quot;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="303c5e6f-95bd-8016-803c-e864c01c9348" class="">if <strong>name</strong> == &quot;<strong>main</strong>&quot;:<br/>main()</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
